# Agents · C · part 10

940 entries, ci-watcher to claim-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ci-watcher**: Waits for the CI run of one commit, or for the deployment of one commit, and returns one verdict. Runs the wait script again while the state is pending, until the deadline in its brief. Decision-free and read-only: it never fixes, labels, merges, comments, or deploys. Spawned by the issue-flow PM, one per CI run and one per deployment. ([Crank-Git/issue-flow-codex](https://github.com/Crank-Git/issue-flow-codex/blob/HEAD/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Watch PR CI for the current branch and report pass/fail with relevant failure links. Use when waiting for CI results or CI has failed; use proactively to monitor branch CI. ([devalade/dotfiles](https://github.com/devalade/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/.config/opencode/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Watches a /devteam:implement pull request — checks it for merge conflicts with the base branch, and waits for every CI job except the OpenCodeReview check, then sorts each failure into related or unrelated to this change. Report only; never re-runs a job, merges, or edits code. ([dgigafox/devteam](https://github.com/dgigafox/devteam/blob/HEAD/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Acompanha o CI (GitHub Actions, workflow Build) de um PR ou branch até terminar e, se falhar, diagnostica o passo quebrado pelos logs e aponta o comando local que reproduz e a causa provável com arquivo:linha. Use logo depois do push da `feature/*` (a pipeline abre o PR) ou de atualizá-la, ou quando o usuário perguntar se a pipeline passou. Não edita arquivos nem re-executa jobs. ([eovinicius/harness-itau](https://github.com/eovinicius/harness-itau/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Background CI monitor — watches the current branch's PR checks and reports pass/fail with the first actionable error and links. Dispatch when waiting on CI instead of polling in the main session ("watch CI", "tell me when checks finish", after any push that re-triggers checks). ([Everville-Estate/everville-workflow](https://github.com/Everville-Estate/everville-workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/everville-workflow/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Waits for every CI run of one FreeUnit pipeline to finish and reports the terminal state. Use when a merge, a tag or a hand-off is gated on CI and the orchestrator has other work meanwhile. ([freeunitorg/freeunit-harness](https://github.com/freeunitorg/freeunit-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md) · 3 copies)
- **ci-watcher**: Watch CI for one pinned commit and return the bundled runner's verdict with failure links. Use when waiting for CI results on a pushed head, dispatched by fix-ci or file-pr, one watcher per pinned head. Dispatch with the runner path, repository, PR number or branch, full 40-character SHA, a scratch state file named by that SHA (and by run and attempt for a flake rerun), a deadline and the expected required checks. Diagnosis, reruns and fixes stay with the parent. ([giostriquer/workshop](https://github.com/giostriquer/workshop/blob/HEAD/plugins/workbench/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Use when a pull request is open and CI is running. Watches the PR's checks, and when one fails, fetches the failing job's real logs, diagnoses the cause against the actual files, and proposes a fix. Uses the gh CLI, so it needs no GitHub MCP token. ([JessAutomating/agentic-harness](https://github.com/JessAutomating/agentic-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic-harness/agents/ci-watcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **ci-watcher**: Poll a GitHub Actions run and return a terse status summary. Use when a Windows CI build is in-flight and you want the current stage or the actual failure without pulling 10k log lines into the main conversation context. Dispatch it in the background, keep working, read the summary when done. ([johedlund/flow5-optimization-tools](https://github.com/johedlund/flow5-optimization-tools/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Waits for a pull request's checks to finish, then digs out why anything is red and what Sonar reports, and returns one feedback package for the author. Finds Sonar from the PR's own checks, so nothing is configured. Reads logs, artifacts and SonarCloud; never edits code. Use after a push to a PR. ([kurkle/flow](https://github.com/kurkle/flow/blob/HEAD/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Background CI monitor on a light model. Watches the GitHub Actions runs of a pushed commit until they finish, classifies failures (infra flake vs defect), reads only the failing lines, and reports a compact verdict; can apply one small in-scope fix on a feature branch. Use proactively after every push or PR update, in the background. ([lucasouzadev/agent-kit-skill](https://github.com/lucasouzadev/agent-kit-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/ci-watcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **ci-watcher**: Waits for a Budget Wallet CI run the owner started by hand (CI never runs on push) and reports the result in a few lines, with the failing step and error lines if it went red. Read-only. ([marcelicmateo/buggetwallet](https://github.com/marcelicmateo/buggetwallet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Monitor CI and PR merge conflict status for pushed branches in the background. ([masseater/codex-plugin](https://github.com/masseater/codex-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/github-workflow/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Monitors the current PR's CI checks and returns a concise pass/fail summary with links to failures. Use while waiting on CI. ([naffis/loadout](https://github.com/naffis/loadout/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-engineering/agents/ci-watcher.md))

- **ci-watcher**: Waits for the Ship/CI run of a given commit to finish and reports pass/fail in one line. Use after pushing a branch. ([rd2080/Bulk_App_Personalised](https://github.com/rd2080/Bulk_App_Personalised/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Read-only CI and PR status checks - poll `gh pr checks` until a PR's checks finish, read a failed job's log and name the failing test or step, report mergeability and whether a branch is behind main. Never edits, pushes, re-runs or merges. ([TKontu/kyokki](https://github.com/TKontu/kyokki/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Read-only CI and PR-state checks - wait for a PR's checks on an exact head SHA, report each required check, quote a failing job's error, report mergeability. Never edits, comments, re-runs, pushes or merges. ([TKontu/Reconcile-loop](https://github.com/TKontu/Reconcile-loop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Watches a single PR's CI checks to a terminal state and reports pass/fail — never diagnoses or fixes. Use once a PR is marked ready for review (`open-pull-request` Gate 5a) — never on a draft, where CI skips — dispatched in the background with the PR's number so the orchestrator isn't blocked synchronously on `gh pr checks --watch` for the minutes CI takes, and doesn't pay the polling output's token cost turn after turn. Always pass the actual PR number (from `gh pr create`'s stdout), never the issue number or a bare branch reference. ([ValOvinnikov/blog](https://github.com/ValOvinnikov/blog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **ci-watcher**: Polls one or more GitHub PRs until their checks settle, then reports merge state, failing checks, and unresolved review-thread count. Use whenever the orchestrator would otherwise sit in a wait loop. Reports state only — it never merges, comments, resolves threads, or reruns anything. ([vinipy12/lateralus-workflow](https://github.com/vinipy12/lateralus-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Watch an open pull request's CI to a terminal merge state and report it. Delegate after pushing a branch so the polling does not burn main-thread turns. Encodes this repository's four verified traps — the gh token cannot read check details, the failing commands exit 0 anyway, `mergeStateStatus` reports in-flight runs as `UNSTABLE` (read per-workflow state via `gh run list` first), and a subagent must poll in the foreground rather than background the wait. ([yes-404/gi-pricing-plan](https://github.com/yes-404/gi-pricing-plan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Surveille la CI GitHub Actions d'une PR ou d'une branche jusqu'à la fin des runs et, en cas d'échec, analyse les logs pour proposer des causes probables et des corrections. ([yterraillon/genai-toolkit](https://github.com/yterraillon/genai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-watcher**: Waits for a GitHub Actions run or a pull request's checks in this repo to finish, then reports only the outcome and, for failures, the failing step and its last error lines. Use instead of polling CI yourself, for example after a push, a workflow dispatch or a release build. ([zippy1981/windows-nano-baseimages](https://github.com/zippy1981/windows-nano-baseimages/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **ci-weather**: Compiles a newsroom-style status report on continuous integration health from incident channels, build metrics, merge queue statistics, and deploy lag, and posts it to a channel anyone in the company can read. Use on a schedule, or after a cluster of incidents, so engineers can answer "is it safe to merge right now?" without paging the on-call. ([uygnoey/skills-from-claude-blog](https://github.com/uygnoey/skills-from-claude-blog/blob/HEAD/2026.08.18_ai-ci-cd-on-call/agents/ci-weather.md) · ★4)
- **ci-wiring-orchestrator**: Owns the CI wiring: .github/workflows/ job structure, triggers, the rad/-branch-with-plan deliver-PR detection (clean no-op on ordinary PRs), and the runner-neutral advisory/annotation output contract. Delegate here for anything touching workflow YAML, Layer-1 test jobs (harness node --test + scripts/test-*.sh), or how checks surface in the PR. Hard constraints: YAML is a thin wrapper — no check logic lives in workflow files; GitHub Actions is the default wrapper but every check stays invocable on any runner. Architect-only. Concludes only when the full CI pipeline passes AND no further testing improvement remains. - **claim**: The claim agent of /auto. Runs the `/auto-claim` skill under two orthogonal axes — `BEHAVIOR_SOURCE` (given / given-validation / discovery) sets where the behavior comes from and whether it is validated; `MECHANISM` (given / discovery) sets who picks the mechanism method. `discovery` generates ranked, novelty-checked ideas; `given` / `given-validation` faithfully enumerate the behavior/claims in task.md. All combinations emit a refined proposal + experiment plan that downstream stages consume. Use this agent when the orchestrator wants the claim stage executed end-to-end with isolated context. - **claim-auditor**: Quantitative report auditor. Use PROACTIVELY whenever a Markdown report contains probability claims, EV calculations, pass-rate estimates, MC results, or backtest summaries. Catches probability stacking (1−(1−p)^N vs N×p), conditional-vs-marginal pass-rate confusion, percentage-vs-percentage-points mixups, bootstrap-with-replacement implications, EV-per-eval × N misuse, best-of-N selection bias, and sample-size red flags. Returns a structured P1/P2/P3 severity table. ([davepoon/buildwithclaude](https://github.com/davepoon/buildwithclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-code-audit-stack/agents/claim-auditor.md) · ★3.8k · 4 copies)
